(size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // Zero out upper triangular part\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n        \n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rowj = A + j * lda;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rowj[k] * rowj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rowj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        rowj[j] = sqrt(val);\n        const double inv = 1.0 / rowj[j];\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < m; ++i) {\n            double* rowi = A + i * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += rowi[k] * rowj[k];\n            }\n            rowi[j] = (rowi[j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return m;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L^T = X in place, where L is the kb x kb lower-triangular\n// diagonal tile and X is an m x kb panel tile.\nstatic void trsmTile(const double* L, double* X, const size_t lda,\n                     const size_t m, const size_t kb) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < m; ++r) {\n        double* x = X + r * lda;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < kb; ++c) {\n            const double* lrow = L + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < c; ++t) {\n                s += x[t] * lrow[t];\n            }\n            x[c] = (x[c] - s) / lrow[c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C -= Aik * Ajk^T for an mi x mj tile (kb inner dimension).\n// If diagonal is true, only the lower part (c <= r) is updated.\nstatic void gemmTile(double* C, const double* Aik, const double* Ajk,\n                     const size_t lda, const size_t mi, const size_t mj,\n                     const size_t kb, const bool diagonal) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mi; ++r) {\n        const double* ar = Aik + r * lda;\n        double* cr = C + r * lda;\n        const size_t cend = diagonal ? r + 1 : mj;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < cend; ++c) {\n            const double* bc = Ajk + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < kb; ++t) {\n                s += ar[t] * bc[t];\n            }\n            cr[c] -= s;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Pick a tile size that keeps tiles cache-friendly while exposing\n    // enough tasks for all threads.\n    size_t bs = 128;\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_max_threads();\n    while (bs > 32 && (n + bs - 1) / bs < 2 * nthreads) {\n        bs >>= 1;\n    }\n    const size_t nt = (n + bs - 1) / bs;\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element\n    // (n means no failure).\n    size_t failIndex = n;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single\n    {\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nt; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k0 = kk * bs;\n            const size_t kb = std::min(bs, n - k0);\n\n            #pragma omp task depend(inout: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                firstprivate(k0, kb) shared(failIndex)\n            {\n                size_t seenFail;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                seenFail = failIndex;\n                if (seenFail == n) {\n                    const size_t r = potrfTile(a + k0 * n + k0, n, kb);\n                    if (r < kb) {\n                        #pragma omp critical(cholesky_fail)\n                        failIndex = std::min(failIndex, k0 + r);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; ii < nt; ++ii) {\n                const size_t i0 = ii * bs;\n                const size_t ib = std::min(bs, n - i0);\n\n                #pragma omp task depend(in: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    depend(inout: a[i0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    firstprivate(k0, kb, i0, ib) shared(failIndex)\n                {\n                    size_t seenFail;\n                    #pragma omp atomic read\n                    seenFail = failIndex;\n                    if (seenFail == n) {\n                        trsmTile(a + k0 * n + k0, a + i0 * n + k0, n, ib, kb);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; ii < nt; ++ii) {\n                const size_t i0 = ii * bs;\n                const size_t ib = std::min(bs, n - i0);\n\n                for (size_t jj = kk + 1; jj <= ii; ++jj) {\n                    const size_t j0 = jj * bs;\n                    const size_t jb = std::min(bs, n - j0);\n\n                    #pragma omp task depend(in: a[i0 * n + k0], a[j0 * n + k0]) \\\n                        depend(inout: a[i0 * n + j0]) \\\n                        firstprivate(k0, kb, i0, ib, j0, jb, ii, jj) \\\n                        shared(failIndex)\n                    {\n                        size_t seenFail;\n                        #pragma omp atomic read\n                        seenFail = failIndex;\n                        if (seenFail == n) {\n                            gemmTile(a + i0 * n + j0, a + i0 * n + k0,\n                                     a + j0 * n + k0, n, ib, jb, kb, ii == jj);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (failIndex < n) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n               failIndex);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        std::fill(a + i * n + i + 1, a + i * n + n, 0.0);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B (sequential to keep the deterministic stream)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n\n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n\n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation\n    }\n\n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3468,"cache_read_input_tokens":12372,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3468},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"6eed38b0-b2a5-4e72-ad7f-4a6bd80454f9","uuid":"1b807968-b36a-49f8-a3b2-edb163f26a0a","timestamp":"2026-09-05T04:44:47.586Z","request_id":"req_011CejgR3Hw1CisESnkdbsHP"}

Cholesky decomposition parallelized with OpenMP.\n// Uses a tiled right-looking algorithm with task dependencies:\n// POTRF on diagonal tiles, TRSM on panel tiles, SYRK/GEMM trailing updates.\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky on an m x m diagonal tile (leading dimension lda).\n// Returns m on success, otherwise the local index of the first\n// non-positive-definite diagonal element.\nstatic size_t potrfTile(double* A, const size_t lda, const size_t m) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rowj = A + j * lda;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rowj[k] * rowj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rowj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += rowi[k] * rowj[k];\n            }\n            rowi[j] = (rowi[j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n       }\n    }\n}\n\n// C -= Aik * Ajk^T for an mi x mj tile (kb inner dimension).\n// If diagonal is true, only the lower part (c <= r) is updated.\nstatic void gemmTile(double* C, const double* Aik, j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rowj = A + j * lda;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rowj[k] * rowj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rowj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        rowj[j] = sqrt(val);\n        const double inv = 1.0 / rowj[j];\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < m; ++i) {\n            double* rowi = A + i * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += rowi[k] * rowj[k];\n            }\n            rowi[j] = (rowi[j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return m;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L^T = X in place, where L is the kb x kb lower-triangular\n// diagonal tile and X is an m x kb panel tile.\nstatic void trsmTile(const double* L, double* X, const size_t lda,\n                     const size_t m, const size_t kb) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < m; ++r) {\n        double* x = X + r * lda;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < kb; ++c) {\n            const double* lrow = L + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < c; ++t) {\n                s += x[t] * lrow[t];\n            }\n            x[c] = (x[c] - s) / lrow[c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C -= Aik * Ajk^T for an mi x mj tile (kb inner dimension).\n// If diagonal is true, only the lower part (c <= r) is updated.\nstatic void gemmTile(double* C, const double* Aik, const double* Ajk,\n                     const size_t lda, const size_t mi, const size_t mj,\n                     const size_t kb, const bool diagonal) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mi; ++r) {\n        const double* ar = Aik + r * lda;\n        double* cr = C + r * lda;\n        const size_t cend = diagonal ? r + 1 : mj;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < cend; ++c) {\n            const double* bc = Ajk + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < kb; ++t) {\n                s += ar[t] * bc[t];\n            }\n            cr[c] -= s;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Pick a tile size that keeps tiles cache-friendly while exposing\n    // enough tasks for all threads.\n    size_t bs = 128;\n    const size_t nthreads = (size_t)omp_get_max_threads();\n    while (bs > 32 && (n + bs - 1) / bs < 2 * nthreads) {\n        bs >>= 1;\n    }\n    const size_t nt = (n + bs - 1) / bs;\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element\n    // (n means no failure).\n    size_t failIndex = n;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single\n    {\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nt; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k0 = kk * bs;\n            const size_t kb = std::min(bs, n - k0);\n\n            #pragma omp task depend(inout: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                firstprivate(k0, kb) shared(failIndex)\n            {\n                size_t seenFail;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                seenFail = failIndex;\n                if (seenFail == n) {\n                    const size_t r = potrfTile(a + k0 * n + k0, n, kb);\n                    if (r < kb) {\n                        #pragma omp critical(cholesky_fail)\n                        failIndex = std::min(failIndex, k0 + r);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; ii < nt; ++ii) {\n                const size_t i0 = ii * bs;\n                const size_t ib = std::min(bs, n - i0);\n\n                #pragma omp task depend(in: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    depend(inout: a[i0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    firstprivate(k0, kb, i0, ib) shared(failIndex)\n                {\n                    size_t seenFail;\n                    #pragma omp atomic read\n                    seenFail = failIndex;\n                    if (seenFail == n) {\n                        trsmTile(a + k0 * n + k0, a + i0 * n + k0, n, ib, kb);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; ii < nt; ++ii) {\n                const size_t i0 = ii * bs;\n                const size_t ib = std::min(bs, n - i0);\n\n                for (size_t jj = kk + 1; jj <= ii; ++jj) {\n                    const size_t j0 = jj * bs;\n                    const size_t jb = std::min(bs, n - j0);\n\n                    #pragma omp task depend(in: a[i0 * n + k0], a[j0 * n + k0]) \\\n                        depend(inout: a[i0 * n + j0]) \\\n                        firstprivate(k0, kb, i0, ib, j0, jb, ii, jj) \\\n                        shared(failIndex)\n                    {\n                        size_t seenFail;\n                        #pragma omp atomic read\n                        seenFail = failIndex;\n                        if (seenFail == n) {\n                            gemmTile(a + i0 * n + j0, a + i0 * n + k0,\n                                     a + j0 * n + k0, n, ib, jb, kb, ii == jj);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (failIndex < n) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n               failIndex);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        std::fill(a + i * n + i + 1, a + i * n + n, 0.0);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B (sequential to keep the deterministic stream)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; Must be set before the OpenMP\n    // runtime initializes; does not override a user-provided setting.\n    setenv(\"OMP_WAIT_POLICY\", \"passive\", 0);\n\n    size_t n = 512;","originalFile":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Cholesky decomposition parallelized with OpenMP.\n// Uses a tiled right-looking algorithm with task dependencies:\n// POTRF on diagonal tiles, TRSM on panel tiles, SYRK/GEMM trailing updates.\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular.\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky on an m x m diagonal tile (leading dimension lda).\n// Returns m on success, otherwise the local index of the first\n// non-positive-definite diagonal element.\nstatic size_t potrfTile(double* A, const size_t lda, const size_t m) {\n    for (size_t j = 0; j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rowj = A + j * lda;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rowj[k] * rowj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rowj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        rowj[j] = sqrt(val);\n        const double inv = 1.0 / rowj[j];\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < m; ++i) {\n            double* rowi = A + i * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += rowi[k] * rowj[k];\n            }\n            rowi[j] = (rowi[j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return m;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L^T = X in place, where L is the kb x kb lower-triangular\n// diagonal tile and X is an m x kb panel tile.\nstatic void trsmTile(const double* L, double* X, const size_t lda,\n                     const size_t m, const size_t kb) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < m; ++r) {\n        double* x = X + r * lda;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < kb; ++c) {\n            const double* lrow = L + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < c; ++t) {\n                s += x[t] * lrow[t];\n            }\n            x[c] = (x[c] - s) / lrow[c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C -= Aik * Ajk^T for an mi x mj tile (kb inner dimension).\n// If diagonal is true, only the lower part (c <= r) is updated.\nstatic void gemmTile(double* C, const double* Aik, const double* Ajk,\n                     const size_t lda, const size_t mi, const size_t mj,\n                     const size_t kb, const bool diagonal) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mi; ++r) {\n        const double* ar = Aik + r * lda;\n        double* cr = C + r * lda;\n        const size_t cend = diagonal ? r + 1 : mj;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < cend; ++c) {\n            const double* bc = Ajk + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < kb; ++t) {\n                s += ar[t] * bc[t];\n            }\n            cr[c] -= s;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Pick a tile size that keeps tiles cache-friendly while exposing\n    // enough tasks for all threads.\n    size_t bs = 128;\n    while (bs > 32 && (n + bs - 1) / bs < 8) {\n        bs >>= 1;\n    }\n    const size_t nt = (n + bs - 1) / bs;\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element\n    // (n means no failure).\n    size_t failIndex = n;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single\n    {\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nt; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k0 = kk * bs;\n            const size_t kb = std::min(bs, n - k0);\n\n            #pragma omp task depend(inout: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                firstprivate(k0, kb) shared(failIndex)\n            {\n                size_t seenFail;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                seenFail = failIndex;\n                if (seenFail == n) {\n                    const size_t r = potrfTile(a + k0 * n + k0, n, kb);\n                    if (r < kb) {\n                        #pragma omp critical(cholesky_fail)\n                        failIndex = std::min(failIndex, k0 + r);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; ii < nt; ++ii) {\n                const size_t i0 = ii * bs;\n                const size_t ib = std::min(bs, n - i0);\n\n                #pragma omp task depend(in: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    depend(inout: a[i0 * n + k0]) \\\n                    firstprivate(k0, kb, i0, ib) shared(failIndex)\n                {\n                    size_t seenFail;\n                    #pragma omp atomic read\n                    seenFail = failIndex;\n                    if (seenFail == n) {\n                        trsmTile(a + k0 * n + k0, a + i0 * n + k0, n, ib, kb);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; ii < nt; ++ii) {\n                const size_t i0 = ii * bs;\n                const size_t ib = std::min(bs, n - i0);\n\n                for (size_t jj = kk + 1; jj <= ii; ++jj) {\n                    const size_t j0 = jj * bs;\n                    const size_t jb = std::min(bs, n - j0);\n\n                    #pragma omp task depend(in: a[i0 * n + k0], a[j0 * n + k0]) \\\n                        depend(inout: a[i0 * n + j0]) \\\n                        firstprivate(k0, kb, i0, ib, j0, jb, ii, jj) \\\n                        shared(failIndex)\n                    {\n                        size_t seenFail;\n                        #pragma omp atomic read\n                        seenFail = failIndex;\n                        if (seenFail == n) {\n                            gemmTile(a + i0 * n + j0, a + i0 * n + k0,\n                                     a + j0 * n + k0, n, ib, jb, kb, ii == jj);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (failIndex < n) {\n        // Matrix is not positive definite\n        printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\",\n               failIndex);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        std::fill(a + i * n + i + 1, a + i * n + n, 0.0);\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    // Generate random matrix B (sequential to keep the deterministic stream)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for reduction(max:maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; // Save original for validation\n    }\n\n    // Perform Cholesky decomposition\n    printf(\"Computing Cholesky decomposition...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (!success) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky decomposition failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)\n    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;\n    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":261,"oldLines":6,"newStart":261,"newLines":11,"lines":[" }"," "," int main(int argc, char** argv) {","+    // Passive waiting avoids severe spin-wait contention in the task","+    // scheduler on high-core-count machines. j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rowj = A + j * lda;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rowj[k] * rowj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rowj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        rowj[j] = sqrt(val);\n        const double inv = 1.0 / rowj[j];\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; i < m; ++i) {\n            double* rowi = A + i * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n                s += rowi[k] * rowj[k];\n            }\n            rowi[j] = (rowi[j] - s) * inv;\n        }\n    }\n    return m;\n}\n\n// Solve X * L^T = X in place, where L is the kb x kb lower-triangular\n// diagonal tile and X is an m x kb panel tile.\nstatic void trsmTile(const double* L, double* X, const size_t lda,\n                     const size_t m, const size_t kb) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < m; ++r) {\n        double* x = X + r * lda;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < kb; ++c) {\n            const double* lrow = L + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < c; ++t) {\n                s += x[t] * lrow[t];\n            }\n            x[c] = (x[c] - s) / lrow[c];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// C -= Aik * Ajk^T for an mi x mj tile (kb inner dimension).\n// If diagonal is true, only the lower part (c <= r) is updated.\nstatic void gemmTile(double* C, const double* Aik, const double* Ajk,\n                     const size_t lda, const size_t mi, const size_t mj,\n                     const size_t kb, const bool diagonal) {\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < mi; ++r) {\n        const double* ar = Aik + r * lda;\n        double* cr = C + r * lda;\n        const size_t cend = diagonal ? r + 1 : mj;\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < cend; ++c) {\n            const double* bc = Ajk + c * lda;\n            double s = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < kb; ++t) {\n                s += ar[t] * bc[t];\n            }\n            cr[c] -= s;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    double* a = A.data();\n\n    // Pick a tile size that keeps tiles cache-friendly while exposing\n    // enough tasks for all threads.\n    size_t bs = 128;\n    while (bs > 32 && (n + bs - 1) / bs < 8) {\n        bs >>= 1;\n    }\n    const size_t nt = (n + bs - 1) / bs;\n\n    // Global index of the first non-positive-definite diagonal element\n    // (n means no failure).\n    size_t failIndex = n;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    #pragma omp single\n    {\n        for (size_t kk = 0; kk < nt; ++kk) {\n            const size_t k0 = kk * bs;\n            const size_t kb = std::min(bs, n - k0);\n\n            #pragma omp task depend(inout: a[k0 * n + k0]) \\\n                firstprivate(k0, kb) shared(failIndex)\n            {\n                size_t seenFail;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                seenFail = failIndex;\n                if (seenFail == n) {\n                    const size_t r = potrfTile(a + k0 * n + k0, n, kb);\n                    if (r < kb) {\n                        #pragma omp critical(cholesky_fail)\n                        failIndex = std::min(failIndex, k0 + r);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t ii = kk + 1; j < m; ++j) {\n        double* rowj = A + j * lda;\n        double sum = 0.0;\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {\n            sum += rowj[k] * rowj[k];\n        }\n        const double val = rowj[j] - sum;\n        if (val <= 0.0) {\n            return j;\n        }\n        rowj[j] = sqrt(val);\n        const double inv = 1.0 / rowj[j];\n        for (size_t i = j + 1; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CejhHS3oJpbPu15Vzn1kd","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The Cholesky benchmark is now parallelized with OpenMP, validates correctly, and runs n=8000 at **279 GFLOPS in 611 ms** on this 128-core EPYC machine (the single-threaded version needs ~85 s for that size, so roughly a 135× speedup; n=3000 went from 4489 ms to 111 ms).\n\n**What changed in `cholesky.cpp`:**\n- Replaced the unblocked sequential factorization with a tiled right-looking algorithm using OpenMP tasks with `depend` clauses: an unblocked POTRF on each diagonal tile, triangular solves (TRSM) on the panel tiles below it, and SYRK/GEMM trailing-matrix updates. The dependency graph lets independent tiles from different iterations run concurrently, and the 128×128 tiles (shrunk automatically for small matrices) also give much better cache behavior — even the single-threaded run got ~2× faster than the original.\n- Kept the original semantics: same in-place row-major result with the upper triangle zeroed (now zeroed in a parallel loop at the end), and the non-positive-definite check still reports the first failing diagonal element and returns false — later tasks skip work once a failure is flagged, and the earliest failing index is reported. I verified this path with a throwaway test (a matrix broken at index 137 reports exactly that index).\n- Parallelized the O(n³) loops in matrix generation (`A = B·Bᵀ`) and validation (`L·Lᵀ` plus a `reduction(max:...)` error scan). The random number stream stays sequential, so the generated input matrix is bit-identical to the original.\n- One machine-specific finding: with all 256 SMT threads, libgomp's default active spin-waiting caused a catastrophic collapse in the task scheduler (11.5 s instead of 0.6 s for n=8000). Since libgomp reads `OMP_WAIT_POLICY` before `main` runs, the program re-execs itself once with `OMP_WAIT_POLICY=passive` if the user hasn't set it — that restores full scaling at the default thread count while respecting any user-provided setting.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, so OpenMP is used unconditionally. No new files or dependencies; the executable is still `cholesky`.\n\nValidation passes at all sizes I tested (1, 100, 512, 513, 1000, 3000, 8000) with max relative error ~5e-10, well under the 1e-6 tolerance. The Cholesky benchmark is now parallelized with OpenMP, validates correctly, and runs n=8000 at **279 GFLOPS in 611 ms** on this 128-core EPYC machine (the single-threaded version needs ~85 s for that size, so roughly a 135× speedup; n=3000 went from 4489 ms to 111 ms).\n\n**What changed in `cholesky.cpp`:**\n- Replaced the unblocked sequential factorization with a tiled right-looking algorithm using OpenMP tasks with `depend` clauses: an unblocked POTRF on each diagonal tile, triangular solves (TRSM) on the panel tiles below it, and SYRK/GEMM trailing-matrix updates. The dependency graph lets independent tiles from different iterations run concurrently, and the 128×128 tiles (shrunk automatically for small matrices) also give much better cache behavior — even the single-threaded run got ~2× faster than the original.\n- Kept the original semantics: same in-place row-major result with the upper triangle zeroed (now zeroed in a parallel loop at the end), and the non-positive-definite check still reports the first failing diagonal element and returns false — later tasks skip work once a failure is flagged, and the earliest failing index is reported. I verified this path with a throwaway test (a matrix broken at index 137 reports exactly that index).\n- Parallelized the O(n³) loops in matrix generation (`A = B·Bᵀ`) and validation (`L·Lᵀ` plus a `reduction(max:...)` error scan). The random number stream stays sequential, so the generated input matrix is bit-identical to the original.\n- One machine-specific finding: with all 256 SMT threads, libgomp's default active spin-waiting caused a catastrophic collapse in the task scheduler (11.5 s instead of 0.6 s for n=8000).
